备注：Figure 3 proposes two connected catalytic cycles: a mainly thermal branch for 1a and 8a, and a photoinduced branch for 6a/8a. Copper complexes A–E/E*, radicals 2a-A/2a-B/1a-A/6a-A/8a-A/8a-B, and borate 3a-A are proposed intermediates, not isolated preparations.
